Source: https://www.gov.uk/guidance/mca-approved-training-providers-atp

## Approved nautical colleges

- [List of MCA approved nautical colleges, yacht deck and small vessel engineer training providers](https://www.gov.uk/government/publications/mca-approved-nautical-colleges-yacht-deck-and-small-vessel-engineer-training-providers)

## Other maritime administrations

The MCA accepts some STCW short courses from other maritime administrations. For more information, please see section 4 of [MIN 643](https://www.gov.uk/government/publications/min-643-m-maritime-and-coastguard-agency-mca-short-course-approval-process-guidance).

We do not accept any online courses where there is a requirement for face-to-face practical training in the STCW Code

## Training provider not listed

### 1). Does the training provider state the course is MCA approved?

If not, it is unlikely to be approved by the MCA. You should instead find out which administration approved this (by checking the course web page or by contacting the training provider) and [check whether we can accept/recognise the course](#non-mca).

### 2). Could the company you found be acting as an intermediary?

The company advertising MCA approved courses may not be the provider delivering the training but are instead acting as a commercial partner. In most cases, the course web page will tell you who the actual training provider is.

# STCW Basic Training - Personal Survival Techniques - MCA Approved Training Providers

Training Providers: If you are an approved MCA course provider and not on this list or if your details need updating please
email: stc.courses@mcga.gov.uk . If you hold pilot approval you will need to wait until full approval is granted by an MCA
surveyor before being listed below.

Seafarers: If you have undertaken or are about to undertake MCA approved training and it is not listed below, please email
exams@mcga.gov.uk to ensure it is MCA approved.

# STCW Training in Advanced Fire Fighting - MCA Approved Training Providers

Training Providers: If you are an approved MCA course provider and not on this list or if your details need updating please
email: stc.courses@mcga.gov.uk . If you hold pilot approval you will need to wait until full approval is granted by an MCA
surveyor before being listed below.

Seafarers: If you have undertaken or are about to undertake MCA approved training and it is not listed below, please email
exams@mcga.gov.uk to ensure it is MCA approved.

In addition to the above, the MCA will accept the following ECDIS, IMO 1.27 courses for Certificate of Competency **Revalidation** only:

- ECDIS certificates issued under the authority of an EU administration and meeting the STCW 2010 standard (we may need to verify the course
criteria which may lead to a delay when processing your application)

- ECDIS Certificates issued under the authority of Australian Maritime Safety Authority (AMSA)

- ECDIS Certificates issued under the authority The New Zealand Maritime school (Manukau Institute of Technology) from 01 January 2011 and
Nelson Marlborough Institute of Technology (NMIT)

- ECDIS Certificate issued by Transport Canada

- ECDIS Certificate issued by the National Maritime College of Ireland (NMCI)

# STCW Updated Proficiency in Personal Survival Techniques - MCA Approved Training Providers

Training Providers: If you are an approved MCA course provider and not on this list or if your details need updating please
email: stc.courses@mcga.gov.uk . If you hold pilot approval you will need to wait until full approval is granted by an MCA
surveyor before being listed below.

Seafarers: If you have undertaken or are about to undertake MCA approved training and it is not listed below, please email
exams@mcga.gov.uk to ensure it is MCA approved.

Source PDF: https://assets.publishing.service.gov.uk/media/5a8ff7fde5274a5e64c54721/Update_on_acceptance_of_Indian_and_Pakistan_Certificates_of_Proficiency.pdf

**Update on acceptance of Indian and Pakistan Certificates of Proficiency**

For the purpose of issuing your first UK Certificates of Competency the MCA will accept
certificates issued under the authority India and Pakistan if they were issued **before** 1 [st]
January 2017.

       Proficiency in Survival Craft and Rescue Boats other than Fast
Rescue Boats,

       Proficiency in Advanced Fire Fighting,

       - Proficiency in Medical First Aid **and**

       Proficiency in Medical Care.

**They will only be accepted up until 1** **[st]** **February 2018** .
